# GW Teams & Builds - extracted 2026-11-10

Copy the folders inside Skills/ into Documents/Guild Wars/Templates/Skills/
File names: <slot> [a/b/c = alternative] <primary-secondary> [note].  A-, B- prefixes = additional sections/variants within the same post.

### Team Hero - Contagion Spike (Updated)

```
Team Hero – Contagion Spike     (Updated)
Updated and improved version.
The build is now more dynamic, with higher DPS, and also has additional survivability.
---
Core Idea
Spread conditions across entire enemy groups using Contagion, creating strong pressure, chain reactions, and massive condition spikes.
And deal damage using Dark Aura.
---
How it works:
Contagion → whenever you gain a new condition, all nearby foes receive the same condition.
,
Fevered Dreams (Mesmer Hero) → spreads new conditions from the target to nearby enemies and can inflict Dazed if multiple conditions are present on the target.
,
This combination creates strong AoE condition pressure and continuous chain spreading across enemy groups.
---
Damage Mechanic
Foul Feast (condition transfer to self from ally)
Transfers all conditions from an allied hero to you, while granting health and energy per condition removed.
This makes it a reliable tool to consistently “trigger” Contagion through yourself.
,
Dark Aura (main damage source)
Deals AoE damage whenever you lose health.
This further strengthens the build’s synergy and damage loops.
,
Improvements in this version:
Higher and more stable DPS
,
More dynamic gameplay flow
,
Anti-knockdown protection
,
Added health regeneration
,
Increased maximum HP via alcohol buffs
,
More controlled condition management through Foul Feast
,
---
Hero Codes:
1) OAZEQYxGqHdzEJkAiTSTuFSHwUB (Sundering annyversaly Scythe "Sufferer" of Enchantment)
2) OQhkAsC8gFKzJY6lDMd40hQG4iB
3) OQhkAsB8gFK0NAC4EAJAHERQGwFD
4) OghjgwMc4QXVLgfDgD5QhD0CXMA
5) OAljUwGopSrBHVVBoBKgbh1Y7YA
6) OAhjQoGYIP3hhWVV4N5imDzxJA
7) OANCY8zkKRCYV1MBUzdlWU9A
8) Owgj0wQc4QeRNgbE3NTfQg+D2EA
⁠ᴛᴇᴀᴍ-ʜᴇʀᴏ-ᴄᴏɴᴛᴀɢɪᴏɴ⁠
```

### For a SoS healer player, the role is a bit too complex, but

```
For a SoS healer player, the role is a bit too complex, but it can be replaced with: OAOjEuiMpRCPTOSTrijTMTXTvgA / OAOjEuiMpRCPTOSTYMjTMTXTvgA Also, you can replace the current SoS with a Monk healer using GDW: OwAS0YITeRNgbE5gTf+Drivg Or a Resto Ritualist healer with GDW: OACiEykMJ8M5INtKSMNOxJexAA If the player wants to play an offensive SoS, then we replace him with one of the ES Mesmers instead. The hero resto setup can be changed to: OACjEuiKpSETYMMTCPTOSTjTciA
```

### Let's look at damage reduction options if the tank is not a

```
Let's look at damage reduction options if the tank is not a warrior with the Defy Pain skill. So that he can survive in a regular team without using the Shadow form. this is an Assassin OwFj0xfzITOMMMHMxgvl6PxZ0kA , or Dervish OgCjkqrLrSmXaXNXcXvlfb0kqiA  In this case, the position of player 2 Rit SOS and player 5 are responsible for reducing damage to the tank. How it looks in practice, SOS absorbs part of the damage with spirits, player 5 focuses on control. That is, the use of Ineptitude and Shared_Burden is strongly recommended for player 5, and it is important to explain his task to him. Also consider using a hybrid monk OwAT02nCVyLqBcjIH8ua6DC8FEA (In this case, it is recommended that another player take the resurrection skill.) What is the team composition in this case:
1) OwFj0xfzITOMMMHMxgvl6PxZ0kA  / OgCjkqrLrSmXaXNXcXvlfb0kqiA / OQASE5JThFJOiFiPvl0kXFqi
2) OAOj4oiMJS0gOOYMMTrilTXTzDA / OAOj4oiMJS0gYMeTMTriXTlTzDA / OAOj4oiMJS0gYMMTrilT+gXTzDA
3) OQZCAswENw0zNlNopjFHyQbJ / OQZCAswENw0zNlNopzQAyQbJ / OQZCAswUIUOwNlNopjFHygGA (When forming a mid lane of mesmers, ideally it should be Pi + SB, but if the player is inexperienced, just replace Pi with Panic + SB)
4) OQZCAswkgz0jNopzGwJgNJyA
5) OQZCAbw0LACYemNIJAyQPVbJ / OQZCAcwEhDCYemNoFHyQPVbJ (What other advantage does SB have, besides reducing damage, it makes the enemy move/attack slower and attack the closest target, if the tank is not experienced, then 1 mesmer SB is definitely your choice!)
6) OAhjQoGYIP3hhWVV4N5kmDzxJA / OAhjQoGYITCPTOSTVV3BjT3ktiA (BIP player)
7) OwAS0YITNgbETf5gXEQgMEvg / OwAS0YITNgeRbE5g+DTfQgvg /OwAT02nCVyLqBcjIH8ua6DC8FEA
8) OACiAykMRDiNZ984EOBNZN5MBA / OACjAyhDJT0gYTWPPOhTQTXMOTA /OAKjYhgMpS0gYTWPPOhTQTQHOTA ( if you use EoE for ST rit, need rune +3 Spawning Power, and staff +60hp)
```

## team_physical-balanced

### Team_Physical balanced_for melee

```
Team_Physical balanced_for melee
Well balanced physical damage team for melee player
Good control, defense, flexible configuration settings and comfortable play.
If you are blocking enemies, it is recommended to turn off MOP as it causes enemies to scatter.
Turn it off Rigor Mortis if enemies don't block, it will speed up the activation Barbs.
Pairs well with Hundred Blades and Vow of Strength

### Team_solo pet for Casters team

```
Team_solo pet for Casters team
The task is to integrate 1 hero with a pet into a team of spellcasters as efficiently as possible.
12 beast mastery 12 marksmanship 13 expertise. So, here I will explain why exactly these hero settings. After all, with 9 expert knowledge, he has enough energy for maniken. Note: Use this configuration if additional pressure reduction is needed against heavy melee damage.
5) OwAT42nC1phgXp+uErf1PgUPAA
6)OwAT42nC1phgXp+uErf1PgUPAA
I removed Holy Wrath from both Monks and replaced it with Aegis to provide party-wide blocking instead of additional damage reflection.
This is entirely optional and generally only useful against very heavy melee pressure. I personally haven't found it necessary in most situations.
If you choose to make this change, it is recommended to run Aegis on both Monks, as the skill performs significantly better when chained by two characters.
p.s. Recommended hero Health: 470–505 HP.
⁠ᴛᴇᴀᴍ-ʙᴜɪʟᴅ-ᴛᴇsᴛ⁠

## team-hero-mop

### Team-hero MoPway_for a melee player (update)

```
Team-hero MoPway_for a melee player (update)
1)  W Hundred Blades /D VoR or any melee
2) OACjEuiMpNXTMm3cZmxmcSzxJA
3) OQhkAsC8gFKDNY6lDMd40hQG4iB
4) OQhkAsC8gFKzJY6lDMd40hQG4iB
5) OQlkAkB8wYmEhDCYeGJAHERQN2OG or OgljswMopSKRTDrDHOQiQD1Y7YA
6) OABDQsZ3O3h5wQzQ8IqqWC7A
7) OAhjYYHbIPPVLxnoqK8mcSzhJA
8) OAOj4ggMpOYTr3jLcCNtmOz5BA
The team needs micro, and good ball. Preferably 2 hotkeys to use 2 MoP to two different targets at the same time, which will increase the damage from Mark of Pain. Despite having SB mesmer, mobs in hard mode still run away, so it is more effective to use water elementalist and/or knockdown to keep the target stationary.   It should also work well to jump to the target with skills such as Death's Charge.
Equipment heroes: ⁠ᴇǫᴜɪᴘᴍᴇɴᴛ-ʜᴇʀᴏᴇs
⁠ᴛᴇᴀᴍ-ʙᴜɪʟᴅ-ᴛᴇsᴛ⁠
```
